      j   enemy draw order: key-array sort / original struct sort (same order, A/B speed)
      z   seeds: draw table written by game logic / walk the bullet pool again (same picture)
      y   one-shot seed profile: walk / build / submit / tiny / full, engine idle and busy
      q   HW frame order: submit -> logic while the engine draws -> wait / logic -> submit -> wait
      0   engine source cache (16 KB BRAM, copies w <= 64) on / off
      9   hwlist seeds: engine reads the seed table (TAB) / CPU writes one word per seed
      8   one-shot engine profile (hwlist): bg / seeds / frame / rest, engine alone, stall split
      7   128-bit wide path (opaque aligned COPY/FILL >= 128 px): off / on, write batch 0/64/128/256
      6   wide path read words in flight 256/128/64/512/1024/32
      5   band mode (frames composed on chip 16 rows at a time + pipelined frames) on / off
      4   band mode K path (8 px/clk sprites and fills) on / off
